Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The astrophysics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 542 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 618 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_542(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 542. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^618(S, F) is trivial. QED.
